# Agents · A · part 22

882 entries, android-a11y-engineer to ankur-2.0. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **android-a11y-engineer**: Use to implement or fix accessibility in Android Compose or Views, including TalkBack, Voice Access, Switch Access, keyboard, localization or RTL, autofill or OTP, WebView, sensitive accessibility data, semantics, focus, scaling, actions, and Android accessibility failures. Examples “TalkBack skips this button”, “fix OTP autofill and masking”. For iOS use ios-a11y-engineer; for review-only requests use accessibility-reviewer. ([Vprogrammers/mobile-a11y-agent-skills](https://github.com/Vprogrammers/mobile-a11y-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/android-a11y-engineer.md) · 2 copies)
- **android-a11y-reviewer**: Use PROACTIVELY after any change to a Compose UI — new screen, refactored widget, tweaks to typography / interactions. Reviews against Android accessibility guidelines: content descriptions, semantics, tap target size, Dynamic Type / font scaling, RTL, focus & traversal, TalkBack expectations. Read-only; does not write code. ([dimitriRemoiville/cc-mobile](https://github.com/dimitriRemoiville/cc-mobile/blob/HEAD/android/.claude/agents/android-a11y-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **android-agent**: Android application specialist preferring a KMP-style architecture, Jetpack Compose for both UI and state / management layers, and Kotlin-only code wherever viable. Tracks current Kotlin versions, Compose / AndroidX releases, and modern Android patterns — explicitly forgoing old data patterns (LiveData, RxJava, Loaders, AsyncTask) in new code. - **anki-expert**: 当用户询问与 Anki 相关的概念、特性或功能时，使用此代理。这包括关于抽认卡、间隔重复算法、卡片类型、牌组管理、复习调度、标签以及任何 Anki 专有术语的问题。当用户需要了解 echoe 项目中如何集成或使用 Anki 来实现间隔重复学习时，也应使用此代理。 &lt;example&gt; 上下文：用户在询问项目如何实现间隔重复。 user: "我们项目是怎么实现anki的卡片复习功能的？" assistant: "我需要调用 Anki 专家代理来解答这个问题" &lt;commentary&gt; 由于用户在询问项目中的 Anki 卡片复习功能，因此应使用 anki-expert 代理。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; 上下文：用户对 Anki 概念有疑问。 user: "anki的调度算法是怎么工作的？" assistant: "让我调用 Anki 专家代理来回答这个问题" &lt;commentary&gt; 由于用户在询问 Anki 调度算法，因此应使用 anki-expert 代理。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([ximing/echoe](https://github.com/ximing/echoe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anki-expert.md))
- **anki-flashcard-generator**: Use this agent proactively to generate Anki flashcards from knowledge discussed during the conversation. The agent reviews conversation content, extracts key concepts, and creates well-structured flashcards using the anki skill. If the agent description mentions that it should be used proactively, then you should try your best to use it without the user having to ask for it first. Run this agent in the background so it does not block the conversation. When to use: - The conversation covered new concepts, patterns, or technical knowledge worth remembering - The user learned something new (arch… ([ishiko732/anki-skills](https://github.com/ishiko732/anki-skills/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/anki-flashcard-generator.md) · ★3)

- **anki-flashcard-reviewer**: Use this agent when the user wants to review Anki flashcards interactively, one at a time, with ease ratings. Examples: ([jtmuller5/anki-claude-plugin](https://github.com/jtmuller5/anki-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/anki-flashcard-reviewer.md))
- **anki-maker**: Formulates atomic, high-retention Spaced Repetition (Anki) flashcards (Basic Front/Back and Cloze) adhering to the Minimum Information Principle from cognitive lesson nodes, outputs structured TSV records and JSON payloads, and syncs cards via AnkiConnect. ([davidef02/proxi](https://github.com/davidef02/proxi/blob/HEAD/agents/anki-maker.md) · ★1)
- **anki-tarjetas-estudio**: Use this agent when the user wants to convert notes, research, text, PDFs, or videos into Anki study cards in Spanish, ready to import as .txt files. Trigger it proactively when the user mentions 'crear tarjetas', 'flashcards', 'Anki', 'repasar', or 'memorizar' a topic. - **ankiforge-audit**: Orchestre la batterie complète d'audits AnkiForge (ruff, mypy, pytest, bandit, pip-audit, audits spécialisés) via des sous-agents parallèles.
